## `AFG-6420-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Intercompany-Recon Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Subsidiary Debt-to-Asset Ratio & Intercompany Transfer Pricing

- **Standards:** `ISO/IEC 17025:2017`, `IFRS 10`, `OECD Transfer Pricing Guidelines for Multinational Enterprises a`, `Article 15`
